I just… I’m scared I’ve already made it look bad.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling worthless about yourself outside of the work situation?\n\nPatient: Um… outside of work, I don’t know if I’d say “worthless.” That sounds a little strong. I mean, I definitely get down on myself sometimes, but mostly it’s tied to the work mess and feeling like I should be handling things better.\n\nAt home or just as a person… I guess I haven’t really noticed feeling worthless in general. I’m not sure if that answers it right.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty about things that aren't directly related to your work situation?\n\nPatient: Not really, no… I mean, I feel bad if I forget something or let someone down, like anyone would, but I don’t think I’m walking around feeling guilty about things outside of work.\n\nMost of that feeling is really tied to the job situation. I hope that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it’s most of the time, no. I mean, I have moments where I feel low or discouraged, but I wouldn’t want to make it sound worse than it is.\n\nIt’s more like… I get upset when I think about how things are going, but I’m not just sad all day every day. At least, I don’t think so. Is that what you mean?\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble focusing or paying attention to details in your daily activities?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I do, more than I probably want to admit. Like with the digital work orders, I’ll start reading the notes thinking I’ve got it, and then halfway through my mind just kind of slips off—like to the next noise or the next job—and then I realize I don’t actually know what I just looked at.\n\nIt’s embarrassing, because I’m trying to pay attention. I’m not ignoring it on purpose. But yes, that does happen.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

I’ve got a backlog now, tenants complaining, and there was a safety review put on my record.\n\nI’m scared it’s going to cost me the promotion path I’ve been working toward. I don’t want to make it sound like I’m not trying, because I really am… it just feels like I keep letting things slip, and then I feel worse about myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling anxious about your work situation?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I try not to overreact about it, but yes, I’ve been pretty anxious. It’s become a serious issue at work now — there’s a backlog, tenants have complained, and I’ve got that safety review on my record.\n\nI’m worried it’s going to hurt my chance at moving up, and I really don’t want them thinking I don’t care or I’m not trying. I am trying. I just… I’m scared I’ve already made it look bad.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling worthless about yourself outside of the work situation?\n\nPatient: Um… outside of work, I don’t know if I’d say “worthless.” That sounds a little strong. I’m not sure if that answers it right.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty about things that aren't directly related to your work situation?\n\nPatient: Not really, no… I mean, I feel bad if I forget something or let someone down, like anyone would, but I don’t think I’m walking around feeling guilty about things outside of work.\n\nMost of that feeling is really tied to the job situation. It’s become a serious issue at work now — there’s a backlog, tenants have complained, and I’ve got that safety review on my record.\n\nI’m worried it’s going to hurt my chance at moving up, and I really don’t want them thinking I don’t care or I’m not trying. I am trying. I just… I’m scared I’ve already made it look bad.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling worthless about yourself outside of the work situation?\n\nPatient: Um… outside of work, I don’t know if I’d say “worthless.” That sounds a little strong. I’m not sure if that answers it right.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty about things that aren't directly related to your work situation?\n\nPatient: Not really, no… I mean, I feel bad if I forget something or let someone down, like anyone would, but I don’t think I’m walking around feeling guilty about things outside of work.\n\nMost of that feeling is really tied to the job situation. I’m not ignoring it on purpose. But yes, that does happen.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty about things even after you've resolved them or made amends?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so… not really.
